   c=conv(p,p); maxc=c.max(); lag=softmax(beta*(c-maxc))
   h = self_gradient_from_lag(p, lag)  # already includes factor 2 maybe
   # g = h (score factor const)

   # Ensure nonnegative finite; if zeros issue: add tiny epsilon? If p zeros, exp factor zero stays zero. To allow reactivation, add floor.

Detailed functions:

`fft_len(m)` next power of two.

`linear_conv(a)`:
```
n=len(a); m=2*n-1; N=1 << (m-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:m]
```

`score_of_mov(a)`:
```
n=len(a); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: return inf
c=linear_conv(a); return 2*n*c.max()/s/s
```

For normalized objective in z, we can compute conv of p (sum1). To save, use p sum1, score =2n*max(c). Need exact for p.

`softmax_z(z)`:
```
z = np.clip(z, -50, 50); z = z - z.max(); e=np.exp(z); return e/e.sum()
```
Bounds if z finite.

`lse_grad_p(p, beta)`:
```
c=linear_conv(p)
cmax=c.max()
scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
exp=np.exp(scaled)
tot=exp.sum()
if tot==0: return cmax, zeros
w=exp/tot
# self gradient h = conv(rev(p), w)[n-1:2n-1]
n=len(p); M=3*n-2; N=1<<...
RAP=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
h=np.fft.irfft(RAP*W,N)[n-1:2n-1]
g=2.0*h
return cmax + np.log(tot)/beta, g, w, c
```
Check `w` length 2n-1, p length n. As earlier h_j=Σ_q p_q w_{j+q}. conv(rev(p), w)[n-1:2n-1], length n. Let's verify for p length3, w delta k=1. We got h = [p1, p0+p2? Wait for self gradient should c1 derivative for p? c1=2p0p1. derivative wrt p0=2p1, p1=2p0, p2=0. Our h with w delta at1: conv(rev(w?) We had h_j=Σ_q p_q w_{j+q}. For j0: p0w0+p1w1+p2w2=p1; j1: p0w1+p1w2+p2w3=p0; j2:p0w2+p1w3=0. g=2h = [2p1,2p0,0], correct. Good.

`lse_grad_z(z,beta)`:
```
p=softmax_z(z)
L,g,_,_=lse_grad_p(p,beta)
mean=np.mean(g)  # sum constraint gradient for softmax chain uses mean? Let's derive.
grad_z = p*(g - mean(g))
return 2*n*L, grad_z
```
Why mean? For softmax p_i = z_i/Z. derivative log Z = p_i. d L/d z_i = Σ_j (dp_j/dz_i) g_j. dp_j/dz_i = p_j(δ_{ij} - p_i). So = p_i(g_i - Σ_j p_j g_j). Should subtract weighted mean Σ p_j g_j, not arithmetic mean. Since sum g weighted by p not all. For non-neg simplex with constraint, use Lagrange λ=Σ p_j g_j. For gradient with respect to z, indeed p_i(g_i - p·g). So subtract weighted mean. If p zeros, fine. If using arithmetic mean not correct. Use `lam = np.dot(p,g)`. But g may have zeros support? Fine.
Objective factor 2n constant.
Potential issue: LSE gradient g includes 2. Good.

```
_OPT_STATE = None
class _Timeout(Exception): pass

def z_objective(z):
    st = _OPT_STATE
    if st is None:
        return (np.inf, np.zeros_like(z))
    t = time.monotonic()
    if t > st['deadline']:
        st['last_z'] = z.copy()
        raise _Timeout
    st['last_z'] = z.copy()
    f, g = lse_score_grad_z(z, st['beta'])
    f *= 1e6; g *= 1e6
    if not np.isfinite(f):
       # fallback: return exact + penalty for negative? z should finite
       f=1e12
       g=np.zeros_like(z)
    # optional clip g
    g = np.clip(g, -1e6, 1e6)
    return f, g
```
But returning g full if f inf? L-BFGS with inf may fail. Use finite: if p sum? softmax handles.

`run_lbfgs_z` code:
```
def run_lbfgs_z(z0, beta, time_budget, maxfun=None, maxiter=5000):
    global _OPT_STATE
    z0=np.clip(z0,-40,40)
    if time_budget<=0: return z0, score_z(z0)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget
    if maxfun is None: maxfun=max(50, int(time_budget*20))
    _OPT_STATE={'beta':beta,'deadline':deadline,'last_z':z0.copy(),'timed_out':False}
    bounds = [(-40.0,40.0)]*len(z0)
    try:
        res = minimize(z_objective, z0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                       options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':30})
        z_out = res.x
    except _Timeout:
        z_out = _OPT_STATE['last_z']
    except Exception:
        z_out = _OPT_STATE['last_z'] if _OPT_STATE else z0
    finally:
        _OPT_STATE=None
    z_out=np.clip(z_out,-40,40)
    return z_out, exact_score_z(z_out)
```
But `z_objective` uses global beta; if exception in other code, okay.
`gtol=1e-8` scaled gradient. Since grad scaled 1e6 and original ~1e-8 =>1e-2; gtol 1e-8 okay. Might run many. `ftol=1e-10` scaled =>1e-16 score, too strict. Set ftol=1e-8 (score 1e-14). Good.

However L-BFGS-B may use finite differences if jac=True? It expects fun returns (f,g). Good.

Pseudo:
```
def eg_polish(p0, time_budget):
  p=normalize(p0); best=p.copy(); best_score=score_p(p); deadline=...
  betas=[10,30,100,300,1000,3000,10000,30000,100000]
  beta_idx=0
  stagn=0
  while time<deadline-2:
     beta=betas[min(idx)]
     improved=False
     for step in range(20, maybe):
        if time>deadline: break
        L,g,_,_ = lse_grad_p(p,beta)
        u = g - np.dot(p,g)
        scale=np.std(u)
        if scale<1e-300: break
        base_score=score_p(p) # maintain
        best_tau=None; best_cand=None; best_sc=base_score
        for tau in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1,3,10]:
            cand=make_eg_candidate(p,u,scale,tau)
            sc=score_p(cand)
            if sc<best_sc-1e-15:
               best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_tau=tau
        if best_cand is not None:
           p=best_cand
           if best_sc < best_score - 1e-15:
              best=p.copy(); best_score=best_sc; stagn=0; improved=True
           continue
        else:
           # no EG improve, try negative tau? or reduce scale? use smaller tau?
           # try exact steepest with beta=inf? Already.
           break
     # after EG steps, pair transfer
     if time<deadline-5:
        p_sc, p = pair_polish(p, budget, best... )
     # update beta: if improved, keep/increase; else try next beta
     if improved:
        # maybe increase beta slightly
        beta_idx=min(len(betas)-1,beta_idx+1)
     else:
        beta_idx=min(len(betas)-1,beta_idx+1)
  # coordinate coordinate golden on p for top gradient
  return best_score,best
```

`generate_simple_component(m, rng, kind)`:
- uniform: ones
- exponential: r^i with r in [0.5,2] normalized, clipped nonneg
- alternating: 1 + α*(-1)^i, α in [0.1,0.9]
- sine: 1 + α cos(2π k i/m + φ), α [0.05,0.8], k 1..m//2
- triangle: min(i, m-i) + small
- anti-pali: pair sums constant: start uniform + random anti differences: t = uniform[-0.5,0.5] scaled; pair a_i=1/(2m)+d_i, a_j=1/(2m)-d_i.
- random positive: exp(normal) maybe.

Let's detail `project_log_additive`:
```
def project_log_additive(a, moduli, iters=30):
    N = int(np.prod(moduli))
    b = resample(a, N)
    x = np.log(b/N + 1e-30)  # centered around 0
    coeff = [np.zeros(m) for m in moduli]
    idx = np.arange(N)
    # optionally initialize coeff to marginal means of x
    for f_i,m in enumerate:
       r=idx%m; coeff[i]=np.bincount(r, weights=x, minlength=m)/ (N/m)
    # iterative
    for it in range(iters):
       pred = np.zeros(N)
       for i,m in enumerate(moduli): pred += coeff[i][idx % m]
       # center: subtract weighted mean maybe 0
       for i,m in enumerate(moduli):
           r=idx % m
           resid = x - (pred - coeff[i][r])
           # counts each residue N/m
           coeff[i] = np.bincount(r, weights=resid, minlength=m) / (N/m)
    pred = sum coeff[i][idx % moduli[i]]
    pred -= np.mean(pred)
    return np.exp(pred), coeff, N
```
Since every residue should have equal count N/m if N divisible by m, yes. For moduli product, each residue has N/m indices. Good.
Clipping pred: before exp, `pred = np.clip(pred, -10, 10)` to avoid overflow; then normalize.
Then refine p. This candidate may have product structure (CART 2/3D). Good.

def eg_polish(p0, deadline, betas=(20,100,500,2000,8000), steps_per_beta=40):
    p = normalize(p0)
    best = p.copy(); best_sc = score_of_p(p)
    for beta in list(betas):

            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1: break
            L,g,c, w = lse_grad_p(p,beta)
            u = g - np.dot(p,g)
            scale = np.std(u)
            if scale < 1e-300: break
            cur_sc = score_of_p(p) # maintain maybe global? To avoid recompute after each step, keep cur_sc variable. We can maintain.

               if sc < best_sc - 1e-15: best=...

def make_eg_candidate(p, u, scale, tau):

def pair_polish(p0, deadline, beta=1000, max_pairs_per_sweep=40, sweeps=None):
   p=normalize(p0); best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_of_p(p)
   sweep=0
   while time<deadline-3:
      cur_score=score_of_p(p)
      L,g,_,_ = lse_grad_p(p,beta)
      u = g - np.dot(p,g)
      order = np.argsort(u)
      low = order[:12]
      high = order[-12:]
      # Build pairs: high donors to low receivers
      candidates=[]
      for j in high:
         if p[j] <= 1e-15: continue
         for l in low:
            if l==j: continue
            candidates.append((j,l))
      # shuffle? deterministic
      # maybe only 40
      pairs Tried=0
      for j,l in candidates:
         if time>=deadline-3 or pairs Tried>=max_pairs...: break
         cand, sc = transfer_golden(p,j,l,best_sc?)
         if sc < cur_score - 1e-15:
             p=cand; cur_score=sc
             if sc<best_sc: best=...
             # recompute u? Could break to recompute after a few improvements
             if (tried+1)%5==0: break
      sweep+=1
      if no improvement and sweep>0: break
   return best,best_sc
```

def transfer_golden(p, j, l):
    original = p.copy()

    cur_best = p.copy(); cur_score = score_of_p(p)

    radius = min(radius, 0.5)
    lo = -min(radius, 0.95*p[j])

def apply_transfer(p,j,l,delta):
    if delta > 0.95*p[j]: return None
    q=p.copy(); q[j]-=delta; q[l]+=delta
    if np.any(q< -1e-15): return None
    q = np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s<=0 return None; return q/s
```

a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
n=len(a)
if n==0: return np.array([])
if n * n <= 200000: return np.convolve(a,a)
m=2*n-1
N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
return np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:m]
```

`lse_grad_p(p,beta)`:
```
n=len(p)
c=fast_self_conv(p)
cmax=float(c.max())
if cmax <=0: return 0, zeros...
scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
# scaled <=0. If beta huge, exp underflows okay.
e=np.exp(scaled)
tot=e.sum()
if not finite or tot<=0: w=np.zeros(2n-1); w[c.argmax()]=1; tot=1; L=cmax
else: w=e/tot; L=cmax + np.log(tot)/beta
# gradient:
N2=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()
p_rev=p[::-1]
Pr=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,N2)
W=np.fft.rfft(w,N2)
h=np.fft.irfft(Pr*W,N2)[n-1:2*n-1]
g=2.0*h
# due numerical, maybe small negative? Conv nonneg but h nonneg if p,w nonneg; ok.
return L,g,w,c
```

def pair_polish(p0, deadline, beta=2000):

       pairs=0

           elif beta < 2000: beta = min(beta*10, 50000)
           sweeps +=1

           # keep beta or increase? increase slightly?
           sweeps +=1
    return best,best_sc

n=len(p); ans=np.zeros(n)
if n==1: return p.copy
for i in range(n//2):
    j=n-1-i
    d = 0.5*(p[i]-p[j])
    val = 0.5/n + d

    # Since pair target sum 1/n, need val in [0,1/n]. Clip.

`log_additive_projection(a, moduli, iters=25)`:
```
N=int(np.prod(moduli))
b=resample_sequence(a,N)
x=np.log(np.maximum(b/N,1e-30))
idx=np.arange(N)
mods=[int(m) for m in moduli]
coeffs=[np.bincount(idx % m, weights=x, minlength=m) / (N/m) for m in mods]
# subtract marginal means to remove base? The sum of coeffs gives prediction; we will center later.
for it in range(iters):
    pred = np.zeros(N)
    for i,m in enumerate(mods): pred += coeffs[i][idx % m]
    for i,m in enumerate(mods):
        r = idx % m
        resid = x - (pred - coeffs[i][r])
        # Each residue count N/m. For moduli not dividing? if product and moduli factors N division divisible by each because N product. Use count = N//m.
        counts = np.bincount(r, minlength=m)
        newc = np.bincount(r, weights=resid, minlength=m) / counts
        coeffs[i]=newc
# pred
pred = sum...
pred -= np.mean(pred)
pred = np.clip(pred, -10, 10)
out=np.exp(pred); out/=out.sum()
return out
```
If b has zeros, log floor. Good.
For m large (3481), `idx % m` array size 7000. Fine.
Counts can vary if residues not equally? They are equal for N product? If m not factor? N product so N/m integer and each residue exactly N/m? For N = prod mods, for one mod m, because other mods product N/m, each residue appears N/m times (assuming idx 0..N-1). Yes.
Potential floating overflow: pred centered, clip ±10. Out sum maybe huge but okay after clip. Normalize.

This projection might produce very spiky if pred large. Clip ±10 enough (1e4 ratio) but normalizing okay. Maybe clip ±5.

Random CRT product: We can implement but maybe skip to keep code simpler. Additive projection of parent to moduli plus random anti candidate enough.

```
def p_space_objective(p,beta,penalty=1.0):
    n=len(p)
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    S=p.sum()
    if S < 1e-300:
       return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
    L,g = lse_grad_p(p,beta)
    F = 2.0*n*L/(S*S) + 0.5*penalty*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    grad = (2.0*n/(S*S))*(g - 2.0*L) + penalty*(S-1.0)*np.ones(n)
    # clip grad to avoid huge
    if not finite: ...
    return F, grad
```

Let's write class `_Timeout(Exception)` top-level.

`run_lbfgs_p`:
```
def run_lbfgs_p(p0,beta,penalty=1.0,time_budget=60.0,maxfun=None,maxiter=5000):
    global _OPT_STATE
    p0=normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    start=time.monotonic()
    if time_budget <=0: return p0, score_internal(p0)
    deadline=start+time_budget
    if maxfun is None:
       maxfun=int(max(50, min(5000, time_budget*20)))
    _OPT_STATE={'beta':float(beta),'penalty':float(penalty),'deadline':float(deadline),'last_x':p0.copy()}
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*len(p0)
    try:
       res=minimize(p_objective, p0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':30})
       x=res.x
    except _Timeout:
       x=_OPT_STATE['last_x']
    except Exception:
       x=_OPT_STATE.get('last_x', p0) if _OPT_STATE else p0
    finally:
       _OPT_STATE=None
    x=normalize_nonnegative(x)
    return x, score_internal(x)
```
If bounds [0,1], and S target1, max coordinate can be 1, okay. But if n large, coordinate 1 sum, others 0; score=2n? bad. L-BFGS won't unless. Good.

But if p from projection has some values >1? normalize sum1, values <1.

For finite but c with negative due FFT roundoff? LSE uses max maybe negative small. L can be negative if all conv negative due numerical? conv of nonnegative should nonnegative, FFT roundoff can be -1e-15. Use `c = np.maximum(fast_self_conv(p),0.0)` in lse_grad and score? Score should not be negative. We can in `fast_self_conv` `out[abs(out)<1e-12]=0`? Use `return np.maximum(np.fft.irfft(...), 0.0)` for convex due roundoff. But conv may have tiny negative at boundaries. Max negative? If all negative tiny, score 0 bad. For nonnegative p, true max positive. Use `c = np.maximum(c,0)`. def fast_self_conv(a):
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   if n < 256:
       out=np.convolve(a,a)
       out[np.abs(out)<1e-15]=0.0
       return out
   m=2*n-1
   N=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:m]
   # remove tiny negative
   out[out < 0.0] = 0.0 if? But if true conv small positive, negative only roundoff. Setting all negative to 0.
   return out
```

`score_internal(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
n=len(a)
if n==0: return inf
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-300: return inf
c=fast_self_conv(a)
if c.size==0: return inf
mc=float(c.max())
if mc <=0: return 0.0
return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```

`normalize_nonnegative(a, target=1.0)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-300:
   return np.full(a.size, target/a.size)
return a*(target/s)
```

`lse_grad_p(p,beta)`:
```
n=len(p)
c=fast_self_conv(p)
cmax=float(c.max())
if cmax <= 1e-300:
   return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
# Option clip lower to -745 to avoid underflow warning? np.exp underflow okay. Use np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -745.0))
e=np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -745.0))
tot=e.sum()
if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 0:
   w=np.zeros(c.size); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; tot=1.0; L=cmax
else:
   w=e/tot
   L=cmax + math.log(tot)/beta if beta>0 else cmax
# gradient
M=3*n-2
N2=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
Pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N2)
W=np.fft.rfft(w, N2)
h=np.fft.irfft(Pr*W, N2)[n-1:2*n-1]
g=2.0*h
# small negatives: h should nonneg but due roundoff; set? g=np.maximum(g,0)? But gradient can have negative? gLSE nonnegative because p,w nonnegative; yes set small negative to 0? However u=g-2L can be negative. g itself nonnegative. Set g[np.abs(g)<1e-15]=0.
return float(L), g
```

`p_space_objective(p,beta,penalty)`:
```
n=len(p); S=p.sum()
if S<1e-300: return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
L,g=lse_grad_p(p,beta)
F=2.0*n*L/(S*S) + 0.5*penalty*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
grad=(2.0*n/(S*S))*(g - 2.0*L) + penalty*(S-1.0)
grad=np.asarray(grad,float)+penalty*(S-1.0)
# Ensure finite
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
   grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
if not np.isfinite(F): return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
return float(F), grad
```

`run_lbfgs_p_continuation(p0,betas,deadline)`:
```
def run_lbfgs_p_continuation(p0,betas,deadline):
    cur=normalize_nonnegative(p0); best=cur.copy(); best_sc=score_internal(cur)
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remaining<=0: return best,best_sc
    per=remaining/len(betas)
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 3: break
       alloc=min(deadline - time.monotonic() - 3.0, max(3.0, per*1.5))
       if alloc < 2: break
       x, sc = run_lbfgs_p(cur, beta, time_budget=alloc, maxfun=int(max(30, alloc*25)), maxiter=3000)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
          best_sc=sc; best=x.copy()
       if sc <= best_sc*(1.0 + 1e-6) or sc < 2.0:
          cur=x.copy()
       else:
          cur=best.copy()
    return best,best_sc
```
Use score <2 threshold okay. But if best_sc 1.5, sc 1.9 maybe still useful? Continue if not much worse: relative 1.005? Use `if sc <= best_sc * 1.02 or sc < best_sc + 0.05`. Good.

def eg_polish(p0, deadline, betas=(200,800,3000,10000,30000), steps_per_beta=60):
   p=normalize(p0); best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_internal(p)
   cur_p=p; cur_sc=best_sc
   tau_list=(0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0)
   for beta in betas:
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5: break
      improved=False
      for step in range(steps_per_beta):
         if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5: break
         L,g = lse_grad_p(cur_p,beta)
         u = g - 2.0*L
         scale = float(np.std(u))
         if not finite or scale <= 1e-300: break
         best_cand=cur_p; best_cand_sc=cur_sc
         for tau in tau_list:
             cand=p_eg_from_u(cur_p,u,scale,tau)
             sc=score_internal(cand)
             if sc < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                 best_cand=cand; best_cand_sc=sc
         if best_cand is not cur_p:
             cur_p=best_cand; cur_sc=best_cand_sc; improved=True
             if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                 best_sc=cur_sc; best=cur_p.copy()
             continue
         else:
             # try stronger explicit tau? already. Break step loop
             break
      if not improved:
          # maybe try one more with lower beta? continue to next
          continue
   return best,best_sc
```

Pair polish as top-level:
```
def pair_polish(p0, deadline, beta=3000, max_pairs=50, samples=8, gold=8):
    p=normalize(p0); best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_internal(p)
    cur=p; cur_sc=best_sc
    sweeps=0
    beta_current=beta
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 and sweeps < 100:
        L,g=lse_grad_p(cur, beta_current)
        u=g - 2.0*L
        order=np.argsort(u)
        low=order[:10]
        high=order[-10:]
        improved=False
        pairs=0
        for j in high:
            jj=int(j)
            if cur[jj] <= 1e-15: continue
            for l in low:
                ll=int(l)
                if jj==ll: continue
                if pairs >= max_pairs or time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                cand, sc = transfer_search(cur, jj, ll, samples=samples, gold=gold, current_score=cur_sc)
                pairs += 1
                if sc < cur_sc - 1e-15:
                    cur=cand; cur_sc=sc; improved=True
                    if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                        best_sc=cur_sc; best=cur.copy()
                    if pairs % 8 == 0:
                        break
            if pairs % 8 == 0:
                break
        if not improved:
           if beta_current > 200:
               beta_current = max(min(beta_current/5.0, 2000.0), 50.0)
           else:
               beta_current = min(beta_current*5.0, 50000.0)
           sweeps += 1
        else:
           # after improvements, maybe keep or slightly increase beta? Keep.
           sweeps += 1
    return best,best_sc
```
Need ensure low/high arrays maybe length less than10 for small n; fine.

`transfer_search`:
```
def transfer_search(p, j, l, samples=8, gold=8, current_score=None):
    if current_score is None: current_score=score_internal(p)
    n=len(p)
    maxv=p.max()
    radius = max(0.05/n, 0.2*max(float(p[j]), float(p[l])), 0.05*maxv)
    radius = min(radius, 0.1)
    lo = -min(radius, 0.95*float(p[j]))
    hi = radius
    if hi <= lo: return p.copy(), current_score
    # sample
    ds = np.linspace(lo, hi, max(1,samples))
    best_d=0.0; best_sc=current_score; best_cand=p
    for d in ds:
        if d == 0.0:
            sc = current_score
        else:
            sc = eval_transfer_score(p,j,l,float(d))
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc=sc; best_d=float(d); q=apply_transfer(p,j,l,float(d)); best_cand=q if q is not None else p
    if best_sc >= current_score - 1e-15:
        return p, best_sc
    # refine interval around best_d
    step=(hi-lo)/max(samples-1,1)
    lo_r=max(lo, best_d - 0.5*step)
    hi_r=min(hi, best_d + 0.5*step)
    if hi_r <= lo_r:
       return best_cand, best_sc
    # golden
    gr=(math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    c1=hi_r - gr*(hi_r-lo_r); c2=lo_r + gr*(hi_r-lo_r)
    sc1=eval_transfer_score(...); sc2=...
    cand1=apply...; cand2...
    # initialize with current best
    full_best_cand=best_cand; full_best_sc=best_sc
    if sc1 < full_best_sc: ...
    if sc2 < full_best_sc: ...
    for _ in range(gold):
       if sc1 < sc2:
          hi_r=c2; c2=c1; sc2=sc1; cand2=cand1; c1=hi_r - gr*(hi_r-lo_r); eval c1...
       else:
          lo_r=c1; c1=c2; sc1=sc2; cand1=cand2; c2=lo_r + gr*(hi_r-lo_r); eval c2...
       update full best
    return full_best_cand, full_best_sc
```
Potential issue: If sample best_d not exactly sample, interval of half-step okay. Golden reeval. If `eval_transfer_score` returns inf, handle by setting score large and cand None.
In golden, if sc1/sc2 inf, interval update may break. We can if both inf break. Use a helper `eval_transfer_q`? Top-level.

Simplify: Since golden code with candidates lengthy. Could use only 12-point sample to avoid complex. But for line search quality, golden good. Let's implement carefully.

Need `apply_transfer` return q or None.
`eval_transfer_score` return inf if None.

`log_additive_projection(a, moduli, iters=30)`:
Need handle moduli product maybe too small? If product <=1, return uniform.
```
def log_additive_projection(a, moduli, iters=30):
   N=int(np.prod([int(m) for m in moduli]))
   if N<=1: return np.array([1.0])
   b=resample_sequence(a,N)
   b=normalize_nonnegative(b)
   x=np.log(np.maximum(b/float(N), 1e-30))
   idx=np.arange(N)
   mods=[int(m) for m in moduli]
   coeffs=[]
   for m in mods:
      r=idx % m
      counts=np.bincount(r, minlength=m).astype(float)
      counts[counts==0]=1
      coeff=np.bincount(r, weights=x, minlength=m)/counts
      coeffs.append(coeff)
   for _ in range(iters):
      pred=np.zeros(N)
      for i,m in enumerate(mods): pred += coeffs[i][idx % m]
      for i,m in enumerate(mods):
         r=idx % m
         resid = x - (pred - coeffs[i][r])
         counts=np.bincount(r, minlength=m).astype(float)
         counts[counts==0]=1
         coeffs[i]=np.bincount(r, weights=resid, minlength=m)/counts
   pred=sum...
   pred -= np.mean(pred)
   pred=np.clip(pred, -8.0, 8.0)
   out=np.exp(pred)
   out=out.sum()? out/=out.sum()
   return out
```
For m=3481,N=6962, counts each 2; good. If N product 7429, idx%m for m=17 counts437. Good.
Potential memory pred N float, okay.
If b has zero, x log floor.

    # maybe extra pair polish if time:

def postprocess(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0.01: return np.full(max(1,len(p)),0.5)
   # zero tiny relative
   p2=p.copy()
   p2[p2 < 1e-12*s] = 0.0
   p2=normalize_nonnegative(p2)
   sc2=score_internal(p2)
   sc1=score_internal(p)
   if sc2 < sc1: p=p2
   else p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
   return p
```

def make_candidates(parent, rng):
    cands=[]
    pnorm=normalize_nonnegative(parent)
    cands.append(pnorm)
    cands.append(anti_pali_const_projection(pnorm))
    cands.append(pali_projection(pnorm))
    n=len(pnorm)
    mods=[]
    if n > 2:
       if n % 2 == 0:
          m=n//2
          if m > 1 and math.gcd(2,m)==1:
             mods.append((2,m))
    mods.append((13,19,29))
    mods.append((17,19,23))
    mods.append((11,23,29))
    for mods_i in mods:
       # limit product size
       prod=1
       for x in mods_i: prod*=int(x)
       if 512 <= prod <= 20000:
          try: cands.append(log_additive_projection(pnorm, mods_i, 25))
          except: pass
    # random additive only if length around product and budget? We don't have budget. Use one.
    try:
       cands.append(random_additive_candidate(mods[-1], rng, 0.25))
    except: pass
    # also anti + random noise? maybe
    return cands
```

`random_additive_candidate` code:
```
def random_additive_candidate(moduli,rng,scale=0.25):
   N=1
   for m in moduli: N*=int(m)
   if N<=1: return np.array([1.0])
   idx=np.arange(N)
   pred=np.zeros(N)
   for m in moduli:
      coeff=rng.standard_normal(int(m))*scale
      pred += coeff[idx % int(m)]
   pred -= np.mean(pred)
   pred = np.tanh(pred*0.5)*4.0 # bounded
   pred = np.clip(pred,-6,6)
   out=np.exp(pred)
   out/=out.sum()
   return out
```
Could be nonnegative. Good.

If c-cmax due roundoff positive? cmax is max, so <=0, but floating maybe small positive 1e-16? `np.maximum(scaled, -745)` no issue; exp might >1 for positive tiny, tot okay. `L=cmax + log(tot)/beta`; if scaled has positives due cmax not exactly? log/tot positive tiny, L slightly >cmax, okay.

Pitfall: `run_lbfgs_p` with bounds [0,1] and p0 sum1. If L-BFGS line search evaluates x with sum not1, penalty. Good.

Pitfall: The objective LSE uses beta; for beta low, L includes logsumexp across all lags potentially large: `log(tot)/beta`; if beta very low 50, c differences ~1e-4, scaled small, tot ~2n ~14000, log~9.5, /50=0.19. L ~cmax+0.19, which is huge compared to max c ~1e-4! Wait c values for p sum1 are ~1/n=1.4e-4, not ~1. `log(tot)/beta = 0.19` (dimension of c) much larger than cmax? That would make L ~0.19, score ~2n*0.19 ~2640, absurd. Did I misunderstand LSE scaling? L = cmax + (1/beta) log Σ exp(beta(c-cmax)). Since c-cmax of order maybe 0 to -1e-4. For beta=50, beta*Δc ~ -0.007, exp ~0.99, sum ~2n=14000, log~9.5, L=cmax+0.19. Yes L far above max! That's because β too small relative to c scale, LSE approximates average? Wait as β→0, LSE(β(c-cmax)) + cmax ≈ cmax + (1/β) log(Σ exp(0)) = cmax + (1/β) log L, which diverges as β→0. That's bad. LSE parameter should be inverse temperature for convex combination; as β→0, it should approach mean, not infinite? Detailed function implementations:

```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 256:
        out = np.convolve(a, a)
        out = np.where(out < 0.0, 0.0, out)
        return out
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    out = np.where(out < 0.0, 0.0, out)
    return out
```
If n=1, m=1, N = 1 << 0 =1, rfft length1, okay.

`score_internal`:
```
def score_internal(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return float('inf')
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size ==0: return float('inf')
    mc=float(c.max())
    if mc <=0.0: return 0.0
    return float((2.0*float(n)*mc)/(s*s))
```

`normalize_nonnegative`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a, target=1.0):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
  a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
  a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
  s=float(a.sum())
  if s <= 1e-300:
      return np.full(a.size, target/float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
  a = a * (target/s)
  return a
```

`lse_grad_p`:
```
def lse_grad_p(p, beta):
  n=p.size if hasattr? p=np.asarray(p,float); n=len
  c=fast_self_conv(p)
  cmax=float(c.max()) if c.size>0 else 0.0
  if cmax <= 1e-300:
      return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
  beta=float(beta)
  if beta <= 0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
      w=np.zeros(c.size); idx=int(c.argmax()); w[idx]=1.0; return cmax, 2.0*? gradient for exact max?
```
For beta<=0 exact max gradient: If one hot at k, g=2h where h_j = a_{k-j}. But we can set beta huge to avoid. If beta<=0, use beta=1e6? Simpler:
```
  raw = beta * float(max(1,n))
  if raw <= 1.0: raw=1.0
  scaled = raw*(c-cmax)
  scaled = np.where(scaled > -745.0, scaled, -745.0)
  e=np.exp(scaled)
  tot=float(e.sum())
  if not finite or tot<=0:
      w=...; L=cmax
  else:
      w=e/tot
      L=cmax + math.log(tot)/raw
  h=...
  g=2*h
  # sanitize g
  g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
  return float(L), g
```
For raw small, L huge maybe but okay. We start beta 10; raw=n*10. No issue.
Need if c.size? yes.
In exact onehot gradient if special? Not needed. But if beta <=0, set raw = 1e9? We can do `if beta <=0: raw=1e12` to approximate max. Simpler:
```
if beta <=0 or not finite: raw = 1e12
else: raw = beta * float(max(1,n))
```
Then scaled for max lags maybe okay (exp for differences underflow). `log(tot)/raw` ~0. Good.

```
M=3*n-2
N2=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
Pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N2)
W=np.fft.rfft(w, N2)
h=np.fft.irfft(Pr*W, N2)[n-1:2*n-1]
g=2.0*h
```

grad = (2.0*n/(S*S))*(g - 2.0*L)

```

`run_lbfgs_p`:
```
def run_lbfgs_p(p0,beta,penalty=1.0,time_budget=60.0,maxfun=None,maxiter=5000):
    global _OPT_STATE
    p0=normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    current_start=time.monotonic()
    if time_budget <= 0:
       return p0, score_internal(p0)
    deadline=current_start+float(time_budget)
    if maxfun is None:
       maxfun=int(max(30, min(6000, float(time_budget)*25.0)))
    _OPT_STATE={'beta':float(beta),'penalty':float(penalty),'deadline':float(deadline),'last_x':p0.copy()}
    n=len(p0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=minimize(p_objective, p0.astype(np.float64), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfun': int(maxfun), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxls':30})
       x=res.x
    except _Timeout:
       x=_OPT_STATE.get('last_x', p0)
    except Exception:
       x=_OPT_STATE.get('last_x', p0) if _OPT_STATE is not None else p0
    finally:
       _OPT_STATE=None
    x=normalize_nonnegative(np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    return x, score_internal(x)
```
If `p0` size huge, bounds memory. Good.

`run_lbfgs_p_continuation` as above. Need pass betas tuple. Use `base = time.monotonic()`? Use deadline absolute.
```
def run_lbfgs_p_continuation(p0, betas, deadline):
    cur=normalize(p0); best=cur.copy(); best_sc=score_internal(cur)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1: return best,best_sc
    remaining0=deadline-time.monotonic()
    per=max(2.0, remaining0 / max(1,len(betas)))
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2: break
       alloc=min(deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0, max(2.0, per*1.4))
       if alloc < 1.5: break
       x,sc=run_lbfgs_p(cur, beta, time_budget=alloc, maxfun=int(max(30, alloc*25)), maxiter=3000)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
           best_sc=sc; best=np.clip(x,0,None).copy()
       if sc <= best_sc + 0.005:
           cur=normalize_nonnegative(x)
       else:
           cur=best.copy()
    return best, best_sc
```
Be careful: if sc < best_sc, best_sc updated before continue condition; condition `sc <= best_sc + 0.005` always true if improved, so cur=x. If worsened by 0.001, cur=x. If worsened >0.005, cur=best. Good.

`make_eg_candidate`:
```
def make_eg_candidate(p,u,scale,tau):
    if scale <=0: return p.copy()
    x = -float(tau)*(u/scale)
    x=np.clip(x,-50.0,50.0)
    cand=p*np.exp(x)
    s=float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return p.copy()
    return cand/s
```

`eg_polish`:
Use `EG_BETAS` constant. But if time_budget small, maybe one beta. The `deadline` absolute. It currently uses `cur_sc`. Need if `lse_grad_p` returns g all zeros, break.
```
def eg_polish(p0, deadline, betas=None, steps_per_beta=60):
    if betas is None: betas=EG_BETAS
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_internal(p)
    cur=p; cur_sc=best_sc
    taus=(0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0)
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5: break
        improved_beta=False
        for _ in range(steps_per_beta):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5: break
            L,g = lse_grad_p(cur, beta)
            u = g - 2.0*L
            scale = float(np.std(u))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
                break
            best_cand=cur; best_cand_sc=cur_sc
            for tau in taus:
                cand=make_eg_candidate(cur,u,scale,tau)
                sc=score_internal(cand)
                if sc < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_cand=cand; best_cand_sc=sc
            if best_cand is not cur:
                cur=best_cand; cur_sc=best_cand_sc; improved_beta=True
                if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc=cur_sc; best=cur.copy()
                continue
            break
        if not improved_beta:
            continue
    return best,best_sc
```
One subtlety: If best_cand is a new array equal to cur but score not improved, we break step. Good. If a tau candidate is cur.copy() (if scale zero or no change), no.
Could `best_cand is not cur` false if no improvement; if `cand` is `cur.copy()` same score not <, no. If improve, true. Good.

`apply_transfer`:
```
def apply_transfer(p,j,l,delta):
    if delta > 0 + 1e-15:
        if delta > 0.98*float(p[j]): return None
    elif delta < 0:
        if delta < -0.98*float(p[l]): return None # Wait if delta negative, p[j]-=dec? Let's define δ positive moving from j to l. If δ negative, it moves from l to j. Then constraints: p[j]-δ, p[l]+δ. For δ negative, p[l]+δ decreases; need δ >= -p[l]. So if delta < -0.98*p[l]: None.
    q=p.copy(); q[j]-=delta; q[l]+=delta
    if np.any(q < -1e-12): return None
    q=np.maximum(q,0.0); s=q.sum(); if s<=0 return None; return q/s
```
But if δ negative, p[j] increases (no upper bound except maybe 1); p[l] decreases. We don't need upper for j because p[j] can increase to >1? Sum1 and p[l] decreases, p[j] could exceed 1 only if p[l] large? If p[j]+|δ| could exceed1, but array can have coordinate >1 (sum1, others positive, max <=1 actually if nonnegative and sum1, coordinate cannot exceed1). If p[j] + |δ| >1, impossible because p[l] decreased at most, sum1, coordinate <=1. Bound [0,1] not needed in transfer. Could allow >1 due floating if p[j] already near1 and δ negative; but then q[j] could >1, score possible but within evaluator. We can clip at 1? If q[j]>1+1e-12, maybe invalid due constraints? Since sum1 nonnegative, q[j]≤1 automatically if others nonnegative, but if q[l]=0 and q[j]>1 others small, still sum<=1? q[j]=1.1 and others 0? sum >1? normalizing after would scale down. We normalize q/s, so >1 fine. No issue.
For δ positive, p[j] decreases; limit. For δ negative, p[l] decreases; limit. Let's implement robust:
```
if delta >=0:
   if delta > 0.98*float(p[j]): return None
else:
   if -delta > 0.98*float(p[l]): return None
q=p.copy(); q[j]-=delta; q[l]+=delta
if q[j] < -1e-12 or q[l] < -1e-12: return None
q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s<=0 return None; return q/s
```
Good.

`eval_transfer_score`:

def eval_transfer_score(p,j,l,delta):
    q=apply_transfer(p,j,l,delta)
    if q is None: return float('inf')
    return score_internal(q)
```

`transfer_search` robust:

def transfer_search(p,j,l,samples=8,golden=8,current_score=None):
    if current_score is None: current_score=score_internal(p)
    n=len(p); maxv=float(p.max())
    radius = max(0.05/float(n), 0.2*max(float(p[j]), float(p[l])), 0.05*maxv)
    radius = min(radius, 0.1)
    lo = -min(radius, 0.98*float(p[j]))
    hi = radius
    if hi <= lo:
        return p.copy(), current_score
    ds = np.linspace(lo, hi, max(1,samples))
    best_cand=p; best_sc=current_score; best_d=0.0
    for d in ds:
       d=float(d)
       if abs(d) < 1e-15:
          sc=current_score
       else:
          sc=eval_transfer_score(p,j,l,d)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
          best_sc=sc; best_d=d
       elif abs(d) < 1e-15 and sc < 0: no
    # Need best_cand for best_d
    if best_d == 0.0: best_cand=p.copy()
    else:
       best_cand=apply_transfer(p,j,l,best_d)
       if best_cand is None: best_cand=p; best_sc=current_score
    if best_sc >= current_score - 1e-15:
       return p.copy(), current_score
    step=(hi-lo)/max(samples-1,1)
    lo_r=max(lo, best_d - 0.5*step); hi_r=min(hi, best_d + 0.5*step)
    if hi_r <= lo_r: return best_cand,best_sc
    gr=(math.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c1=hi_r - gr*(hi_r-lo_r); c2=lo_r + gr*(hi_r-lo_r)
    def? can't nested
    # implement inline with a helper top-level? Need update multiple variables without nested.
```
We need evaluate c1,c2 and loop. We can write a small loop with explicit states variables. In Python, updating sc1,cand1 each iteration okay.
```

    sc2=eval_transfer_score(p,j,l,c2)
    cand1 = None if not finite? apply_transfer

    ...
    for _ in range(golden):
       if sc1 == float('inf') and sc2 == float('inf'): break
       if sc1 < sc2:
           hi_r = c2
           c2 = c1
           sc2 = sc1
           c1 = hi_r - gr*(hi_r - lo_r)
           sc1 = eval_transfer_score(p,j,l,c1)
       else:
           lo_r = c1
           c1 = c2
           sc1 = sc2
           c2 = lo_r + gr*(hi_r - lo_r)
           sc2 = eval_transfer_score(p,j,l,c2)
       if sc1 < best_sc:
           q=apply_transfer(p,j,l,c1)
           if q is not None: best_sc=sc1; best_cand=q
       if sc2 < best_sc:
           q=apply_transfer(p,j,l,c2)
           if q is not None: best_sc=sc2; best_cand=q
    return best_cand, best_sc
```

`pali_projection`:
```
def pali_projection(p):
  p=normalize_nonnegative(p); n=len; out=zeros
  for i in range(n//2): j=n-1-i; v=0.5*(p[i]+p[j]); out[i]=v; out[j]=v
  if n%2: out[n//2]=p[n//2]
  return normalize_nonnegative(out)
```

`anti_pali_const_projection`: deterministic; use small epsilon? maybe no.
```
def anti_pali_const_projection(p):
  p=normalize(p); n=len; out=zeros
  target=1.0/floor(n)
  half=target/2
  for i in range(n//2):
    j=n-1-i
    d=0.5*(p[i]-p[j])
    lim=0.49*half
    if d>lim: d=lim
    elif d<-lim: d=-lim
    out[i]=half+d; out[j]=half-d
  if n%2: out[n//2]=target
  return normalize(out)
```

`postprocess_sequence`:
```
def postprocess_sequence(p):
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    s = p.sum()
    p2 = p.copy()
    p2[p2 < 1e-12 * max(1.0, s)] = 0.0
    p2 = normalize_nonnegative(p2)
    sc1=score_internal(p); sc2=score_internal(p2)
    if sc2 < sc1:
        p=p2
    return p
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Method overview:
- Work with probability-normalized sequences (sum = 1) for local searches.
- Use a relative-temperature log-sum-exp (LSE) smooth approximation of the peak
  self-convolution, with raw temperature beta * n so that the smoothing is
  scale-correct for sum-1 sequences.
- Optimize with L-BFGS-B on the nonnegative box plus a mild sum penalty, and
  with exponentiated-gradient line searches on the simplex.
- Polish with exact 1D pairwise transfer line searches.
- Generate diverse restarts by palindromic / anti-palindromic projections and
  log-additive CRT products (rank-1/few-factor projections of the parent, or
  random additive factors over coprime moduli).
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time
import math

__all__ = ["propose_candidate"]


class _Timeout(Exception):
    pass


_OPT_STATE = None
_MAX_N = 12000

EG_BETAS = (10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0)
LBFGS_BETAS = (10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0)


def _rank_key(item):
    return item[0]


def fast_self_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution using FFT when beneficial."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 256:
        out = np.convolve(a, a)
        out = np.where(out < 0.0, 0.0, out)
        return out
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:m]
    out = np.where(out < 0.0, 0.0, out)
    return out


def score_internal(a):
    """Exact objective, same formula as evaluate_sequence but for arrays."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mc = float(c.max())
    if mc <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float((2.0 * float(n) * mc) / (s * s))


def normalize_nonnegative(a, target=1.0):
    """Clip to [0, 1000], remove NaN/inf, normalize to target sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, target / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)


def lse_grad_p(p, beta):
    """
    LSE-smooth peak of self-convolution and gradient wrt p.

    beta is dimensionless: raw_temperature = beta * max(1, len(p)).
    This avoids the poor scaling that occurs for probability sequences whose
    convolution values are O(1/n).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        raw = 1e12
    else:
        raw = beta * float(max(1, n))

    scaled = raw * (c - cmax)
    scaled = np.where(scaled > -745.0, scaled, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    tot = float(e.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(c.argmax())] = 1.0
        L = cmax
    else:
        w = e / tot
        L = cmax + math.log(tot) / raw

    # Gradient of self-convolution LSE:
    # If c_k = sum_i p_i p_{k-i}, then d c_k / d p_i = 2 p_{k-i}
    # for feasible i.  Hence d L / d p_i = 2 sum_k w_k p_{k-i}.
    need = 3 * n - 2
    nfft = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], nfft)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Pr * W, nfft)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = 2.0 * h
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(L), g


def p_space_objective(p, beta, penalty=1.0):
    """
    Objective for L-BFGS-B:
        score(p) = 2 n LSE(conv(p,p)) / sum(p)^2
    plus a small penalty to keep sum(p) near 1.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L, g = lse_grad_p(p, beta)
    F = (2.0 * n * L) / (S * S) + 0.5 * penalty * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    grad = (2.0 * n / (S * S)) * (g - 2.0 * L)
    if penalty != 0.0:
        grad = grad + penalty * (S - 1.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(F), grad


def p_objective(x):
    """Scipy objective for temporary global optimizer state."""
    st = _OPT_STATE
    if st is None:
        return (np.inf, np.zeros_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)))

    if time.monotonic() > st["deadline"]:
        st["last_x"] = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        raise _Timeout()

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    st["last_x"] = x
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    F, g = p_space_objective(x, float(st["beta"]), float(st["penalty"]))
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(len(x), dtype=np.float64)
    return F, np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)


def run_lbfgs_p(p0, beta, penalty=1.0, time_budget=60.0, maxfun=None, maxiter=5000):
    """Run L-BFGS-B with bounds [0,1] and a timeout."""
    global _OPT_STATE
    p0 = normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    if time_budget <= 0:
        return p0, score_internal(p0)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(time_budget)
    if maxfun is None:
        maxfun = int(max(30, min(6000, float(time_budget) * 25.0)))

    _OPT_STATE = {
        "beta": float(beta),
        "penalty": float(penalty),
        "deadline": float(deadline),
        "last_x": p0.copy(),
    }
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * p0.size

    try:
        res = minimize(
            p_objective,
            p0.astype(np.float64),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-9,
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        x = res.x
    except _Timeout:
        x = _OPT_STATE.get("last_x", p0)
    except Exception:
        x = _OPT_STATE.get("last_x", p0) if _OPT_STATE is not None else p0
    finally:
        _OPT_STATE = None

    x = normalize_nonnegative(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    return x, score_internal(x)


def run_lbfgs_p_continuation(p0, betas, deadline):
    """Continuation over increasing LSE sharpness."""
    cur = normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_sc = score_internal(cur)

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
        return best, best_sc

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    per = max(2.0, remaining / max(1, len(betas)))

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        alloc = min(deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0, max(2.0, per * 1.4))
        if alloc < 1.5:
            break

        x, sc = run_lbfgs_p(
            cur,
            beta,
            penalty=1.0,
            time_budget=alloc,
            maxfun=int(max(30, alloc * 25)),
            maxiter=3000,
        )

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best = np.clip(x, 0.0, None).copy()

        # Continue from the L-BFGS point if it is close, even if exact score
        # did not improve yet, to allow a smoother continuation path.
        if sc <= best_sc + 0.005:
            cur = normalize_nonnegative(x)
        else:
            cur = best.copy()

    return best, best_sc


def make_eg_candidate(p, u, scale, tau):
    """Exponentiated-gradient candidate p * exp(-tau * u / scale)."""
    if scale <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    x = -float(tau) * (u / scale)
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
    cand = p * np.exp(x)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return p.copy()
    return cand / s


def eg_polish(p0, deadline, betas=None, steps_per_beta=60):
    """
    Exponentiated-gradient searches with exact acceptance.  The direction is
    the simplex gradient of the LSE-smoothed score.
    """
    if betas is None:
        betas = EG_BETAS

    p = normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_internal(p)

    cur = p
    cur_sc = best_sc
    taus = (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5:
            break
        improved_beta = False

        for _ in range(steps_per_beta):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5:
                break

            L, g = lse_grad_p(cur, beta)
            u = g - 2.0 * L
            scale = float(np.std(u))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
                break

            best_cand = cur
            best_cand_sc = cur_sc

            for tau in taus:
                cand = make_eg_candidate(cur, u, scale, tau)
                sc = score_internal(cand)
                if sc < best_cand_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_cand_sc = sc

            if best_cand is not cur:
                cur = best_cand
                cur_sc = best_cand_sc
                improved_beta = True
                if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = cur_sc
                    best = cur.copy()
                continue
            break

        if not improved_beta:
            continue

    return best, best_sc


def apply_transfer(p, j, l, delta):
    """
    Transfer mass by delta from coordinate j to coordinate l.
    delta > 0: j decreases, l increases.
    delta < 0: l decreases, j increases.
    """
    if delta >= 0.0:
        if delta > 0.98 * float(p[j]):
            return None
    else:
        if -delta > 0.98 * float(p[l]):
            return None

    q = p.copy()
    q[j] -= delta
    q[l] += delta

    if q[j] < -1e-12 or q[l] < -1e-12 or np.any(q < -1e-12):
        return None

    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return q / s


def eval_transfer_score(p, j, l, delta):
    q = apply_transfer(p, j, l, delta)
    if q is None:
        return float("inf")
    return score_internal(q)


def transfer_search(p, j, l, samples=8, golden=8, current_score=None):
    """Exact line search for a two-coordinate mass transfer."""
    if current_score is None:
        current_score = score_internal(p)

    n = p.size
    maxv = float(p.max())
    radius = max(0.05 / float(n),
                 0.2 * max(float(p[j]), float(p[l])),
                 0.05 * maxv)
    radius = min(radius, 0.1)

    lo = -min(radius, 0.98 * float(p[j]))
    hi = radius
    if hi <= lo:
        return p.copy(), current_score

    ds = np.linspace(lo, hi, max(1, samples))
    best_cand = p.copy()
    best_sc = current_score
    best_d = 0.0

    for d in ds:
        d = float(d)
        if abs(d) < 1e-15:
            sc = current_score
        else:
            sc = eval_transfer_score(p, j, l, d)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_d = d

    if abs(best_d) < 1e-15:
        best_cand = p.copy()
    else:
        q = apply_transfer(p, j, l, best_d)
        if q is not None:
            best_cand = q
        else:
            best_cand = p.copy()
            best_sc = current_score

    if best_sc >= current_score - 1e-15:
        return p.copy(), current_score

    step = (hi - lo) / max(1, samples - 1)
    lo_r = max(lo, best_d - 0.5 * step)
    hi_r = min(hi, best_d + 0.5 * step)
    if hi_r <= lo_r:
        return best_cand, best_sc

    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    c1 = hi_r - gr * (hi_r - lo_r)
    c2 = lo_r + gr * (hi_r - lo_r)

    sc1 = eval_transfer_score(p, j, l, c1)
    sc2 = eval_transfer_score(p, j, l, c2)

    if sc1 < best_sc:
        q = apply_transfer(p, j, l, c1)
        if q is not None:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_cand = q
    if sc2 < best_sc:
        q = apply_transfer(p, j, l, c2)
        if q is not None:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_cand = q

    for _ in range(golden):
        if sc1 == float("inf") and sc2 == float("inf"):
            break
        if sc1 < sc2:
            hi_r = c2
            c2 = c1
            sc2 = sc1
            c1 = hi_r - gr * (hi_r - lo_r)
            sc1 = eval_transfer_score(p, j, l, c1)
        else:
            lo_r = c1
            c1 = c2
            sc1 = sc2
            c2 = lo_r + gr * (hi_r - lo_r)
            sc2 = eval_transfer_score(p, j, l, c2)

        if sc1 < best_sc:
            q = apply_transfer(p, j, l, c1)
            if q is not None:
                best_sc = sc1
                best_cand = q
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            q = apply_transfer(p, j, l, c2)
            if q is not None:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_cand = q

    return best_cand, best_sc


def pair_polish(p0, deadline, beta=200.0, max_pairs=60, samples=8, golden=8):
    """
    Pairwise transfer polishing.  Donors/receivers are chosen from an LSE
    gradient ranking, and each candidate pair is optimized by exact 1D search.
    """
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_internal(p)
    cur = p
    cur_sc = best_sc

    current_beta = float(beta)
    sweeps = 0
    max_sweeps = 200

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 and sweeps < max_sweeps:
        L, g = lse_grad_p(cur, current_beta)
        u = g - 2.0 * L
        order = np.argsort(u)

        low = order[:10]
        high = order[-10:]

        improved = False
        pairs = 0

        for j in high:
            jj = int(j)
            if cur[jj] <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for l in low:
                ll = int(l)
                if jj == ll:
                    continue
                if pairs >= max_pairs or time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
                    break

                cand, sc = transfer_search(
                    cur, jj, ll,
                    samples=samples,
                    golden=golden,
                    current_score=cur_sc,
                )
                pairs += 1

                if sc < cur_sc - 1e-15:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_sc = sc
                    improved = True
                    if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                        best_sc = cur_sc
                        best = cur.copy()
                    if pairs % 8 == 0:
                        break
            if pairs % 8 == 0:
                break

        sweeps += 1
        if not improved:
            if current_beta > 50.0:
                current_beta = max(50.0, current_beta / 3.0)
            else:
                current_beta = min(5000.0, current_beta * 3.0)

    return best, best_sc


def resample_sequence(a, m):
    """Linear resampling to length m."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1.0, m)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)


def pali_projection(p):
    """Project onto palindromic sequences."""
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n = p.size
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n // 2):
        j = n - 1 - i
        v = 0.5 * (p[i] + p[j])
        out[i] = v
        out[j] = v
    if n % 2 == 1:
        out[n // 2] = p[n // 2]
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def anti_pali_const_projection(p):
    """
    Project onto anti-palindromic deviations around constant pair sums.
    For pair (i, j): a_i + a_j = 1/n, with difference inherited from p.
    """
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n = p.size
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    target = 1.0 / float(n)
    half = 0.5 * target
    lim = 0.49 * half

    for i in range(n // 2):
        j = n - 1 - i
        d = 0.5 * (p[i] - p[j])
        if d > lim:
            d = lim
        elif d < -lim:
            d = -lim
        out[i] = half + d
        out[j] = half - d

    if n % 2 == 1:
        out[n // 2] = target

    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def log_additive_projection(a, moduli, iters=30):
    """
    Project log(a) onto a low-order additive model over residues modulo each
    factor.  For coprime moduli this reconstructs a CRT product of factors.
    """
    moduli = [int(m) for m in moduli]
    N = 1
    for m in moduli:
        N *= m
    if N <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    b = resample_sequence(a, N)
    b = normalize_nonnegative(b)

    # b has sum 1; subtract the uniform log baseline.
    x = np.log(np.maximum(b / float(N), 1e-30))
    idx = np.arange(N, dtype=np.int64)

    residues = []
    counts = []
    for m in moduli:
        r = idx % m
        residues.append(r)
        cnt = np.bincount(r, minlength=m).astype(np.float64)
        cnt[cnt == 0] = 1.0
        counts.append(cnt)

    coeffs = []
    for i, m in enumerate(moduli):
        r = residues[i]
        c = np.bincount(r, weights=x, minlength=m) / counts[i]
        coeffs.append(c)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        pred = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        for i, r in enumerate(residues):
            pred += coeffs[i][r]

        for i, m in enumerate(moduli):
            r = residues[i]
            residual = x - (pred - coeffs[i][r])
            coeffs[i] = np.bincount(r, weights=residual, minlength=m) / counts[i]

    pred = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    for i, r in enumerate(residues):
        pred += coeffs[i][r]

    pred -= float(pred.mean())
    pred = np.clip(pred, -8.0, 8.0)
    out = np.exp(pred)
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def random_additive_candidate(moduli, rng, scale=0.25):
    """Random CRT-product candidate with a bounded additive log model."""
    moduli = [int(m) for m in moduli]
    N = 1
    for m in moduli:
        N *= m
    if N <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    idx = np.arange(N, dtype=np.int64)
    pred = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in moduli:
        coeff = rng.standard_normal(m) * float(scale)
        pred += coeff[idx % m]

    pred -= float(pred.mean())
    pred = np.tanh(0.5 * pred) * 3.0
    pred = np.clip(pred, -6.0, 6.0)
    out = np.exp(pred)
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def make_candidates(parent, rng):
    """Create a small pool of diverse initial / restart candidates."""
    parent = normalize_nonnegative(parent)
    cands = [parent]

    cands.append(anti_pali_const_projection(parent))
    cands.append(pali_projection(parent))

    n = parent.size
    mods = []
    if n > 2 and (n % 2 == 0):
        m = n // 2
        if m > 1 and math.gcd(2, m) == 1:
            mods.append((2, m))

    mods.append((13, 19, 29))
    mods.append((17, 19, 23))
    mods.append((11, 23, 29))

    for mset in mods:
        prod = 1
        for m in mset:
            prod *= int(m)
        if 256 <= prod <= 20000:
            try:
                cands.append(log_additive_projection(parent, mset, iters=30))
            except Exception:
                pass

    if mods:
        try:
            cands.append(random_additive_candidate(mods[-1], rng, scale=0.30))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return cands


def refine_candidate(p0, time_budget, tag=""):
    """
    Multi-stage refinement:
       EG line search -> L-BFGS continuation -> pair polish -> final L-BFGS
       -> final short pair polish.
    Returns best exact sequence and score found within budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(time_budget))

    p = normalize_nonnegative(p0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_internal(p)

    # Stage 1: exponentiated gradient.
    eg_dead = min(deadline, t0 + 0.30 * time_budget)
    if time.monotonic() < eg_dead - 2.0:
        try:
            p1, sc1 = eg_polish(best, eg_dead)
            if sc1 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc1
                best = p1.copy()
                print(f"    [{tag}] EG -> {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Stage 2: L-BFGS continuation.
    lbfgs_dead = min(deadline, t0 + 0.65 * time_budget)
    if time.monotonic() < lbfgs_dead - 3.0:
        try:
            p2, sc2 = run_lbfgs_p_continuation(best, LBFGS_BETAS, lbfgs_dead)
            if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc2
                best = p2.copy()
                print(f"    [{tag}] L-BFGS -> {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Stage 3: exact pair transfer polish.
    pair_dead = min(deadline, t0 + 0.90 * time_budget)
    if time.monotonic() < pair_dead - 3.0:
        try:
            p3, sc3 = pair_polish(best, pair_dead, beta=300.0, max_pairs=60)
            if sc3 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc3
                best = p3.copy()
                print(f"    [{tag}] pair1 -> {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Stage 4: final high-sharpness L-BFGS.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
        try:
            alloc = deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0
            p4, sc4 = run_lbfgs_p(best, 3000.0, time_budget=alloc, maxfun=int(max(30, alloc * 25)))
            if sc4 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc4
                best = p4.copy()
                print(f"    [{tag}] final L-BFGS -> {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Stage 5: last short pair polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        try:
            p5, sc5 = pair_polish(best, deadline, beta=1000.0, max_pairs=40)
            if sc5 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc5
                best = p5.copy()
                print(f"    [{tag}] pair2 -> {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_sc


def postprocess_sequence(p):
    """Final sanitation and light zeroing of numerically irrelevant mass."""
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    p2 = p.copy()
    p2[p2 < 1e-12] = 0.0
    p2 = normalize_nonnegative(p2)
    sc1 = score_internal(p)
    sc2 = score_internal(p2)
    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc1:
        return p2
    return p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Main search entry point.  Uses at most the supplied wall-clock budget and
    returns a valid list of non-negative heights.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 12.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        val = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if val is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(val, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 16 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)) and float(arr.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.size < 64:
        parent = normalize_nonnegative(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
    elif parent.size < 1024:
        parent = normalize_nonnegative(resample_sequence(parent, 2048))
    elif parent.size > _MAX_N:
        parent = normalize_nonnegative(resample_sequence(parent, _MAX_N))

    best_p = parent.copy()
    best_sc = score_internal(best_p)
    print(f"Initial parent: n={best_p.size}, score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Build and rank initial candidates.
    candidates = make_candidates(best_p, rng)
    candidates.append(best_p.copy())

    scored = []
    for cand in candidates:
        c = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        if c.size == 0:
            continue
        if c.size > _MAX_N:
            c = normalize_nonnegative(resample_sequence(c, _MAX_N))
        sc = score_internal(c)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, c))

    scored.sort(key=_rank_key)
    selected = [c for _, c in scored[:4]]
    if not selected:
        selected = [best_p]

    # Short refinement of several diverse candidates.
    init_total = max(30.0, min(0.35 * max(1.0, deadline - t_start), 650.0))
    init_deadline = t_start + init_total
    per_init = init_total / max(1, len(selected))

    for i, cand in enumerate(selected):
        if time.monotonic() >= init_deadline - 10.0:
            break
        alloc = min(per_init + 5.0, init_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break
        try:
            p_ref, sc_ref = refine_candidate(cand, alloc, tag=f"init{i}")
            if sc_ref < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc_ref
                best_p = p_ref.copy()
                print(f"  [NEW BEST] init{i}: {best_sc:.10f} n={best_p.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Reserve time for an exact final pair-polish pass.
    reserve = max(120.0, 0.10 * max(1.0, deadline - t_start))
    final_refine_dead = deadline - reserve

    if time.monotonic() < final_refine_dead - 20.0:
        try:
            alloc = final_refine_dead - time.monotonic()
            p_ref, sc_ref = refine_candidate(best_p, alloc, tag="main")
            if sc_ref < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc_ref
                best_p = p_ref.copy()
                print(f"  [NEW BEST] main: {best_sc:.10f} n={best_p.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Explicit final exact-polish section.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        try:
            p_pair, sc_pair = pair_polish(
                best_p,
                deadline - max(10.0, time_budget_s=max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.1)),
                beta=1000.0,
                max_pairs=120,
                samples=10,
                golden=10,
            )
            if sc_pair < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc_pair
                best_p = p_pair.copy()
                print(f"  [NEW BEST] final pair: {best_sc:.10f} n={best_p.size} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If time remains, try a final high-beta L-BFGS and then a short pair pass.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        try:
            alloc = deadline - time.monotonic() - 12.0
            if alloc > 8.0:
                p_lb, sc_lb = run_lbfgs_p(best_p, 5000.0, time_budget=alloc, maxfun=int(alloc * 20))
                if sc_lb < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc_lb
                    best_p = p_lb.copy()
                    print(f"  [NEW BEST] last L-BFGS: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        try:
            p_pair, sc_pair = pair_polish(best_p, deadline, beta=2000.0, max_pairs=50)
            if sc_pair < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc_pair
                best_p = p_pair.copy()
                print(f"  [NEW BEST] final short pair: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final validation / fallback.
    out_p = postprocess_sequence(best_p)
    out_list = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in out_p]
    if sum(out_list) < 0.01:
        out_list = [0.5] * 64

    verified = float("inf")
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if (not np.isfinite(verified)) or (verified > best_sc + 1e-9):
        fallback_p = parent if parent is not None else np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        fallback_p = postprocess_sequence(fallback_p)
        fb_list = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in fallback_p]
        fb_ver = float("inf")
        try:
            fb_ver = float(evaluate_sequence(fb_list))
        except Exception:
            fb_ver = float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(fb_ver) and fb_ver < verified:
            out_list = fb_list
            verified = fb_ver
        else:
            out_list = [0.5] * 64

    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, n={len(out_list)}, time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s", flush=True)
    return out_list
```
